# PD2's patch table against our ports

Answers [#1817](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1817). PD2's pet AI changes were missed ([#1790](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1790), [summon-ai](summon-ai.md)) because nobody checked PD2's own code patches against what we ported. This page walks every record of `ProjectDiablo.dll`'s patch table and every function PD2's start-up installers write into a 1.13c table. It maps each one to the feature it changes and records our status. Static reads only; nothing was run in game.

**Build.** The patch table was parsed from the installed `C:\Games\PD2\ProjectD2\ProjectDiablo.dll` (sha256 `e7eec256…`), file offset `0x49b028`-`0x4a0744`. That is 1115 records of 20 bytes `{dll, offset, value or target, type, length}` ([plrmodes](plrmodes.md)). PD2.gpr's older build (`2ff6c327…`, table at VA `0x1049d028`) holds the same 1115 records with the same offsets, types and lengths; only the PD2 target addresses differ. The tables below give PD2 addresses in the installed build (PD2.gpr's sit `0x10` to `0x70` lower), except the installer section, which gives PD2.gpr's. 1.13c addresses are RVAs of the PD2 install's 1.13c `D2Game.dll` (`24b293b8…`), `D2Client.dll` and `D2Common.dll`.

**Record format.**
- **Module ids.** `dll` 0 is D2Client, 2 is D2Common and 3 is D2Game: a call rewrite's offset lands on an `e8` rel32 in that module in 338 of 350 D2Game, 331 of 342 D2Client and 50 of 55 D2Common records. The apply's name table (PD2.gpr `0x1049ab90`, 8-byte rows) confirms them and names the rest: 1 D2CMP, 4 D2Gfx, 5 D2Lang, 6 D2MCPClient, 7 D2Net, 8 D2Sound, 9 D2Win, 10 Fog, 11 Ijl11, 12 Storm, 13 D2Gdi, 14 D2DDraw, 15 D2Direct3D, 16 D2Glide, 17 D2Multi, 18 D2Launch, 19 SmackW32, 20 Binkw32, 21 Bnclient. Each row holds the name pointer at +0 (`0x1049ab90`) and the module base, filled at run time, at +4 (`0x1049ab94`) ([Records outside the parsed range](#records-outside-the-parsed-range)).
- **Type 1.** Rewrite the rel32 at `offset`, usually a call's, to `target`. Most targets are small register-to-stack thunks (`0x103a…`) that call the real PD2 function; the tables give that function.
- **Type 0.** Write `length` copies of `value`'s low byte (the apply `memset`s them). With length 0, it writes the 32-bit `value`: a pointer into a 1.13c function table, or a larger buffer size.

**Method.**
1. A script parsed the records, found the 1.13c function around each site (Ghidra, PD2.gpr), followed the thunks and grouped the records into units: one per redirected callee and PD2 target, or per run of adjacent bytes.
2. Each unit was read: the decompiled PD2 function against the vanilla code it replaces.
3. Each unit was checked against `plugin/`, `docs/research/`, `docs/pd2/`, `docs/deviations.md`, `docs/roadmap.md` and `docs/features/`, by address and by name.

Status is one of five:
- **ported**: the code or data that does it.
- **deviation**: a `docs/deviations.md` row covers it.
- **missing**: a GitHub issue now tracks it.
- **unclear**: read only partly (not fully decoded), so whether D2R needs it is not settled; #1979 tracks the decode.
- **not needed**: client-only work D2R already does, a feature cut on the roadmap, or no behaviour change. The row says which.

"Undecoded" rows are read only partly. Their issues ([#1847](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1847) and [#1979](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1979) for the extra-records rows) ask for one issue per real rule once decoded.

## Summary

The status counts are not kept here: every PR that changes a row would conflict on them. The parsed range holds 1115 records, plus 1375 records of the same array outside the parsed range and the 65 records of the no-PlugY array ([Records outside the parsed range](#records-outside-the-parsed-range)). To count a status, count the rows in the tables below whose status column holds it (for example `grep -c "| missing |"`, adjusting for the table's column), and the records and units by summing the rows' record and unit cells.

By module: D2Game 615 records, D2Client 410, D2Common 86, D2Lang 3, D2Win 1. A few features account for most of the records:
- PD2's client sound engine: 261 D2Client records. It is a deviation: D2R's sound tables are kept.
- The character save and trade buffer raises (`0x2000` to `0x20000`, for PD2's bigger stash): about 150 D2Game records. Not needed: D2R saves its own stash.
- The 1.13c object class limit (`0x23d`): a deviation, under the row for PD2's blank `<reserved>` objects rows 573-583.

The installer writes (section [Installer table writes](#installer-table-writes)) number 257:
- 129 are ported, named by our code or, for 23 skill entries, by function number ([#1849](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1849) triage);
- 37 are named only by a doc, whose verdict stands;
- 1 is still a real gap ([#1872](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1872)) and 1 is a deviation (srvdo 44, [#1869](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1869)); [#1869](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1869) (think), [#1870](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1870), [#1871](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1871) and [#1873](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1873) are ported and counted above;
- 89 were decoded by [#1849](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1849): 8 missing ([#2001](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2001)-[#2006](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2006)), 12 deviations, 46 not needed, 3 ported and 20 still unclear.

## Missing features and their issues

Filed by this audit, each a sub-issue of [#1817](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1817) (verdicts from the triage on PR [#1850](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1850)):
- [#1828](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1828): item sockets also roll on set items, and only for non-stackables, throwing weapons and quivers.
- [#1829](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1829): magic items get both affixes at high item level. Data fix: the Progressive Affixes companion's `[magic]` 255 to 65/85/90.
- [#1830](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1830): aura-enchanted bosses take their aura from PD2's list at level mlvl/7.
- [#1831](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1831): Frenzy stacks in the srvdo 9 helper.
- [#1832](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1832): new characters' starting item creator.
- [#1833](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1833): Telekinesis no longer picks up arrows and bolts.
- [#1834](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1834): skill start gate, frame+5 event and cooldown packets. For class skills in their class list the gate needs no port: D2R 3.x keeps a cooldown per skill (`localdelay`). Left: packet 0x83, the frame+5 event, the shared timer for off-class skills (oskills, charges) and the in-town skip; Werewolf/Werebear's `globaldelay` was blanked ([#1996](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1996)).
- [#1835](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1835): summons' first think delay and the Revive path.
- [#1837](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1837): curses keep full duration on monsters in Nightmare and Hell.
- [#1838](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1838): max level (99) marker states.
- [#1839](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1839): missile pierce rolled at creation and capped (`pierce_idx`).
- [#1842](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1842): NPC vendors in map levels (Gheed's map store).
- [#1843](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1843): 2H Phase Blade roll.
- [#1844](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1844): stun shortened on hit; monster get-hit end event. Unclear: the stun part is PvP only; the get-hit end event is open.
- [#1846](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1846): per-level ambient light for levels 109-137. Unclear: the byte is levels.txt `Intensity`; whether D2R's HD renderer uses it is unknown.
- [#1847](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1847): decode the remaining D2Game patches. Decoded into [#1861](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1861) (Chill also lowers cast rate), [#1862](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1862) (Dragon Talon kick damage), [#1863](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1863) (pick up from up to 6 away), [#1864](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1864) (half freeze duration), [#1865](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1865) (stun length rules). The second decode added [#1987](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1987) (Redemption skips pet corpses), [#1988](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1988) (summon first think at 6 frames), [#1989](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1989) (pet leash before every think), [#1990](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1990) (mercenary weapon types), [#1991](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1991) (auras restart on gear change), [#1992](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1992) (0-charge item skills stay listed), [#1993](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1993) (no anim-rate refresh while charging), [#1994](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1994) (Sacrifice corpse explosion), [#1995](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1995) (monster mana steal scaled down). The rows still unclear go to [#1979](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1979).
- [#1848](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1848): decode the remaining D2Common patches. Decoded into [#1866](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1866) (crafted item requirements), [#1867](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1867) (dual wield attack speed), [#1868](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1868) (elemental mastery on missiles), [#1998](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1998) (crafted item colour), [#1999](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1999) (shapeshift skills under Delirium or the Cow King set).
- [#1849](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1849): check the installer table writes no doc names. Decoded into [#1869](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1869) (Blade Sentinel), [#1870](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1870) (Frozen Armor and Enchant on allies), [#1871](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1871) (Lieutenant of Sin), [#1872](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1872) (Rathma's spire), [#1873](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1873) (Unsummon drops the Iron Golem's item), and by the decode into [#2001](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2001) (Blood Golem heal), [#2002](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2002) (Raven count), [#2003](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2003) (monster curse list), [#2004](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2004) (no slow on prime evils), [#2005](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2005) (descdam tooltip lines), [#2006](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2006) (Dragon Tail state); 20 rows stay unclear.
- [#1979](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1979): decode the extra records' undecoded rows. Decoded into [#2008](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2008) (Holy Sword parry without block animation), [#2009](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2009) (leech halved on missile hits), [#2010](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2010) (to-hit formula), [#2011](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2011) (NextHit keyed by owner and [#2018](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2018) (Sacrifice on-hit effects), [#2019](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2019) (Grim Ward soft corpses), [#2020](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2020) (map and uber portals on room free), [#2021](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2021) (portal travel check), [#2022](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2022) (just-portaled 15 frames), [#2023](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2023) (quest drops per game), [#2024](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2024) (srvdo 182 at skill start), [#2025](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2025) (monster life per player), [#2026](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2026) (XP factor at levels 20-24), [#2027](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2027) (gold cap 5,000,000), [#2028](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2028) (shorter skill sequences), [#2029](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2029) (Anya personalizes rares and sets), [#2030](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/2030) (Skeleton Key Unlimited). #1817 holds 100 sub-issues (GitHub's limit), so #2024 on are sub-issues of #1979. Rows still unclear stay on #1979.
- [#1851](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1851): Exploding and Poison shrines drop throwing potions (`tpgs`, `tpfs`).
- [#1852](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1852): blank-rarity unique rows weigh 0 (Embersworn drops for us). Data fix: Embersworn `spawnable` 1 to 0.

Closed by triage as close enough (now not needed):
- [#1840](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1840): frame advance and frame-event scheduler. Close enough: the 0x90 cap and event 4 are vanilla's; D2R `0x491240` runs the same loop.
- [#1841](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1841): monster 991's leap hold. Close enough: monster 991 is `uberancientbarb3`, cut content.
- [#1845](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1845): 5 or 20 stat and skill points per click. Close enough: D2R already has bulk stat assignment; only the 5/20 steps differ.

Already tracked, not refiled:
- [#1803](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1803): the mercenary think.
- [#1800](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1800): the Shadow Warrior, including its leash skip at `+0xac550`.
- [#1144](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1144): `.set-home`'s start level.

The other pet issues, [#1801](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1801) and [#1802](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1802), map to the ported or tracked summon rows. [#1795](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1795) (the shared-hook sigscan) has no record here.

## Open questions

- Answered by [#1874](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1874): the records [item-durability](../research/items/item-durability.md) cites (D2Game `+0xec4f`, `+0x13033`, `+0xa16be`) sit before the range parsed here, and all three are ported ([Item durability and throwing stacks](#item-durability-and-throwing-stacks)).
- The extra records' "unclear" rows were decoded by [#1979](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1979); the rows still marked unclear say what is left (most need D2R's matching function or a live PD2 check).
- [ir3-combat-stats](../research/items/ir3-combat-stats.md) calls `0x103046a0` dead code, but D2Common's missile fill (`+0x6bd1a`, `+0x6bf87`) is routed to it here.
- The table-base globals the installers use (`0x105ddeb0` and the others) are filled at run time through `0x103a5f20(module, offset)`. The table names below come from w37's reads and from which of our docs name the functions, not from those offsets.

## Patch records by feature

Each row lists its record count, then each site. A D2Game call site is the `e8` byte; the record's offset is one byte later.

## Records outside the parsed range

Added by [#1874](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1874). Static reads of the installed `ProjectDiablo.dll` (capstone) and PD2.gpr (Ghidra). Addresses are the installed build's unless marked PD2.gpr; the array, its builder and the second array sit at the same address in both builds.

**Where the records live.**
- The array the tables above parse is longer. PD2's start-up routine (PD2.gpr `0x10344580`) passes `0x1049c380` to the apply (`mov ecx`, installed `0x1034467c`, PD2.gpr `0x1034466c`). The apply (installed `0x10344050`, PD2.gpr `0x10344040`) walks 20-byte records until `dll` 27, which is the terminator at `0x104a8608`. That is 2490 records: 162 before the parsed range (`0x1049c380`-`0x1049d028`), the 1115 above, and 1213 after it (`0x104a2744`-`0x104a8608`).
- The records from `0x104a2738` on are zero in the file. The CRT initializer `0x10115820` (entry `0x10403164` of the initializer list) builds them at load time: 6073 dword writes up to `0x104a861c`. The first 30 values are bytes of a constant, taken through installed `0x1037c080` (PD2.gpr `0x1037c010`, byte `edx` of `ecx`), and some are element addresses (`&array[k]`). No other code writes the array.
- A second array, at `0x10435440` (65 records, terminator at `0x10435954`), goes to the same apply from installed `0x103447a8` (PD2.gpr `0x1034479d`), but only when the command line has no `-plugy` (flag PD2.gpr `0x105b1c94`).
- Both calls of the apply are covered. Its record layout is `{dll, offset, value, type, length}`:
  - the module base is row `dll` of the 8-byte table PD2.gpr `0x1049ab94`;
  - type 1 turns `value` into a rel32;
  - length 0 writes 4 bytes; length n writes n copies of the low byte;
  - each write is read back and compared, and a mismatch stops the walk.

**Reading the rows.**
- Units are grouped as in the tables above. A NOP run that a later record overwrites with a call stays a separate line (`+0x3a710`, `+0x66800`, `+0x31940`).
- Many PD2 call targets are lazy stubs: `0x103a5f20 (module, value)` resolves a 1.13c function. Its module enum is its own: 0 D2Client, 1 D2Common, 2 D2Gfx, 3 D2Lang, 4 D2Win, 5 D2Net, 6 D2Game, 7 D2Launch, 8 Fog, 9 Bnclient, 10 Storm, 11 D2CMP, 12 D2Multi, 13 D2MCPClient, 14 D2Sound. A negative value is an ordinal. Row 0 of the stub's two-entry table is 1.13c's: for `+0x4f970` and `+0xe1d90` it is the callee itself.
- A row marked "unclear" was read only to the patched instruction or the called function.

Werewolf (223), Werebear (228) and Vampire Form (388) (PD2 raw skills rows) always take this path | not needed | for class skills in their class list, no behaviour change ([#1834](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1834)): D2R 3.x has its own per-skill cooldown. The merged skills rows hold PD2's `delay` as `localdelay` (Blizzard 23, Meteor 13), and the server's post-start step `0x436c70` (also `0x43acb0`) sends `localdelay` to `0x439500` (state 185 `cooldown`, stat 359 `skill_cooldown` with the skill as layer) and only `globaldelay` to `0x439470` (state 121 `skilldelay`). Differences: (a) Werewolf and Werebear (PD2 raw 223, 228) carry D2R's own `globaldelay` 25 (PD2's rows have only `delay` 18) ([#1996](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1996)), so shifting sets the shared state 121 and blocks delay skills for 25 frames, where PD2's forms take their own slot (`0x10373100`; `0x102fc280` checks ids 0xe4, 0xdf, 0x184); (b) PD2's per-class list `0x1037fd70` (`0x10375480(class)`, 0xc stride): a skill whose row charclass is not the player's class, or not in the list, falls back to one shared flag at player data `+0x1cc` (oskills and charges share one timer in PD2; separate in D2R); (c) PD2 skips the cooldown when `0x10309650(unit)` and `0x1030a7c0(...)` are both non-zero (looks like in town; unproven). All PD2 addresses are PD2.gpr's build ([#1984](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1984)). ## Installer table writes

PD2's start-up routine `0x10344580` runs installers that write PD2 functions into 1.13c tables ([w37](../research/pd2/w37-server-rules.md)). PD2 addresses here are PD2.gpr's; the installed ProjectDiablo.dll is a different build ([#1984](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1984)): its functions sit `+0x10` on below `0x1035xxxx` (up to `0x1034c6b0`) and `+0x70` on from `0x1035xxxx` (from `0x10356f30`) (matched on their first 32 bytes). Status: **ported** (our code names the function), **documented** (a doc names it; its verdict stands), **missing** (a real gap with its own issue), **deviation** (an existing deviation row covers it), **not needed** (no rule D2R lacks, or the feature is cut), **unclear** (decoded only in part by [#1849](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/issues/1849); the evidence says what is left). The D2Client server-message table writes (`0x1036be80`) are PD2's own packets and go with the features above.
